Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The terrestrial operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 204. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 10 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on terrestrial structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 204. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 10. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a terrestrial algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 64. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 837/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 64 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the terrestrial structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 837. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
